                        }),
                        h === "connected"

                      ],
                    }),

                }),

            _ &&

"bg-[#0f172a] text-white border-[#0f172a]" : k.type === "error" ? "bg-red-600 text-white border-red-600" : "bg-white text-[#0f172a] border-amber-200"}`,
